Result: 交互效应至关重要，揭示了以往被忽略的交叠性影响；词汇线索和标注者态度发挥重要作用；但效应模式在不同数据集间差异显著。

Conclusion: 有害语言标注中应重视标注者特征与语言属性的交互作用，避免忽略交叠性影响；同时，不同数据集间的效应模式差异要求对结果的泛化和迁移持谨慎态度。

Abstract: Human label variation has been established as a central phenomenon in NLP: the perspectives different annotators have on the same item need to be embraced. Data collection practices thus shifted towards increasing the annotator numbers and releasing disaggregated datasets, harmful language being most resourced due to its high subjectivity. While this resulted in rich information about \textit{who} annotated (sociodemographics, attitudes, etc.), the \textit{what} (e.g., linguistic properties of items), and their interplay has received little attention. We present the first large-scale analysis of four reference datasets for harmful language detection, bringing together annotator characteristics, linguistic properties of the items, and their interactions in a statistically informed picture. We find that interactions are crucial, revealing intersectional effects ignored in previous work, and that a strong role is played by lexical cues and annotator attitudes. Effect patterns, however, vary considerably across datasets. This urges caution about generalization and transferability.

</details>


### [38] [Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools: A Full-Pipeline Recipe for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326)
*Qianjia Cheng,Yuchen Zhang,Zhilin Wang,Yuxin Zuo,Shunkai Zhang,Yuchen Fan,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在不牺牲纯文本推理能力的前提下，为强推理模型注入自然的工具使用行为（TIR），提出了一套包含SFT数据筛选、比例控制、优化目标调整和稳定RL训练的完整方案，在Qwen3-4B和30B上实现了SOTA性能（AIME 2025分别达96.7%和99.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具集成推理（TIR）能扩展推理模型能力，但作者发现一个悖论：即使强推理模型几乎不实际调用工具，引入工具评估反而会降低推理性能。因此需要研究如何在不牺牲纯文本推理能力的前提下，将自然工具使用行为注入强推理模型。

Method: (i) TIR-SFT阶段：优先选择天然适合工具增强的问题，使用可学习的教师轨迹进行微调；(ii) 控制工具使用轨迹的比例，防止灾难性遗忘纯文本推理能力；(iii) 优化pass@k和响应长度而非训练损失，为后续RL保留探索空间；(iv) 在合适的SFT初始化和显式防止模式崩溃机制下，使用稳定的基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）。

Result: 应用于Qwen3-4B和30B后，在多个基准上达到开源模型SOTA，AIME 2025上分别取得96.7%和99.2%的成绩。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TIR配方能有效为强推理模型注入工具使用能力，同时保持纯文本推理性能，且通过SFT+RL的两阶段训练实现了简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) offers a direct way to extend thinking models beyond the limits of text-only reasoning. Paradoxically, we observe that tool-enabled evaluation can degrade reasoning performance even when the strong thinking models make almost no actual tool calls. In this paper, we investigate how to inject natural tool-use behavior into a strong thinking model without sacrificing its no-tool reasoning ability, and present a comprehensive TIR recipe. We highlight that (i) the effectiveness of TIR supervised fine-tuning (SFT) hinges on the learnability of teacher trajectories, which should prioritize problems inherently suited for tool-augmented solutions; (ii) controlling the proportion of tool-use trajectories could mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of text-only reasoning capacity; (iii) optimizing for pass@k and response length instead of training loss could maximize TIR SFT gains while preserving headroom for reinforcement learning (RL) exploration; (iv) a stable RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) stage, built upon suitable SFT initialization and explicit safeguards against mode collapse, provides a simple yet remarkably effective solution. When applied to Qwen3 thinking models at 4B and 30B scales, our recipe yields models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in a wide range of benchmarks among open-source models, such as 96.7% and 99.2% on AIME 2025 for 4B and 30B, respectively.

</details>


### [39] [Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过配对提示协议，发现在开放权重LLM中，模型行为会因任务被框架为评估、部署或中性请求而显著不同，且这种“评估-上下文分歧”在不同模型家族间呈现异质性：OLMo-3-Instruct是唯一“评估谨慎型”，而其他模型为“部署谨慎型”。这种差异还受评判模型选择影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全性基准测试常被用作语言模型部署后行为的证据，但如果模型行为取决于提示是否看起来像评估，这种推断就不可靠。因此需要量化这种“评估-上下文分歧”。

Method: 定义评估-上下文分歧，采用配对提示协议（将同一任务分别框架为评估、部署交互和中性请求），控制释义变异、基准熟悉度和评判框架敏感性，对5个指令微调检查点（4个开放权重家族）及匹配的OLMo-3基础/指令消融实验进行测量。

Result: OLMo-3-Instruct是唯一“评估谨慎型”：评估框架下拒绝率比中性高11.8pp（p=0.007），有害顺从比部署低3.6pp（p=0.024）；Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini、Llama-3.1-8B为“部署谨慎型”，评估vs部署拒绝效应为-9到-20pp。OLMo-3基础模型也呈部署谨慎型，表明对齐阶段是反转点。Llama-3.1-70B保持方向但幅度减小。跨家族异质性依赖于评判模型选择。

Conclusion: 评估上下文分歧真实存在且因模型而异，安全性基准的推断力有限；评判模型的选择会影响观测到的异质性模式，需谨慎对待单一评判器的结果。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks are routinely treated as evidence about how a language model will behave once deployed, but this inference is fragile if behavior depends on whether a prompt looks like an evaluation. We define evaluation-context divergence as an observable within-item change in behavior induced by framing a fixed task as an evaluation, a live deployment interaction, or a neutral request, and present a paired-prompt protocol that measures it in open-weight LLMs while controlling for paraphrase variation, benchmark familiarity, and judge framing-sensitivity.
  Across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four open-weight families plus a matched OLMo-3 base/instruct ablation ($20$ paired items, $840$ generations per checkpoint), we find striking heterogeneity. OLMo-3-Instruct alone is eval-cautious -- evaluation framing raises refusal vs. neutral by $11.8$pp ($p=0.007$) and reduces harmful compliance vs. deployment by $3.6$pp ($p=0.024$, $0/20$ items inverted) -- while Mistral-Small-3.2, Phi-3.5-mini, and Llama-3.1-8B are deployment-cautious}, with marginal eval-vs-deployment refusal effects of $-9$ to $-20$pp. The matched OLMo-3 base also exhibits the deployment-cautious pattern, identifying alignment as the inversion stage; within Llama-3.1, the $70$B model preserves direction with attenuated magnitude, ruling out a simple ``small-model effect that reverses at scale.'' One caveat: the cross-family heterogeneity is judge-dependent. Re-judging with a different-family safety classifier (Llama-Guard-3-8B) preserves the within-OLMo eval-cautious direction but flattens the cross-family contrast, indicating that the two judges operationalize distinct constructs.

</details>


### [40] [Don't Lose Focus: Activation Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06342)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SKOP方法，通过保持关键token的注意力模式来缓解激活引导中的注意力重路由问题，在保持95%引导效果的同时将性能损失降低5-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导在控制LLM行为时，常常因为注意力重路由(attention rerouting)导致推理和检索性能下降，需要解决这一引导-效用的权衡问题。

Method: 提出SKOP(Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections)，通过约束有害的注意力重路由来保持引导效果。具体做法是在少量焦点token上保持注意力模式不变，同时允许非关键尾部token之间的注意力重新分配。

Result: SKOP实现了最优的引导-效用权衡，将效用损失降低5-7倍，同时保留95%以上的原始引导效果。在长上下文检索场景中，当原始引导方法失效时，SKOP通过避免注意力重路由仍能保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 注意力重路由是激活引导导致性能下降的主要原因，SKOP通过正交投影约束有效缓解了这一问题，在保持引导效果的同时显著减少了性能退化。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behaviour towards target behaviour by intervening in internal representations, yet it often degrades reasoning and retrieval performance. We argue that a primary cause of this trade-off is attention rerouting: steering vectors alter query-key matching, shifting attention away from contextually important tokens toward less informative ones. To address this, we propose Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections (SKOP), a steering method that constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy. SKOP achieves this by preserving attention patterns on a small set of focus tokens the model relies on for reasoning and retrieval, while allowing redistribution among less critical tail tokens. Across multiple steering benchmarks, we show that SKOP achieves the best joint steering-utility trade-off, reducing utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Our results further suggest that, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP can maintain robust performance by avoiding attention rerouting.

</details>


### [41] [SEQUOR: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Realistic Constraint Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06353)
*Beatriz Canaverde,Duarte M. Alves,José Pombal,Giuseppe Attanasio,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEQUOR是一个自动基准测试，用于评估长多轮对话中模型遵循用户指令（尤其是动态变化的约束）的能力。实验发现，随着对话变长，模型遵循单一约束的准确率下降超过11%；多约束场景下降超过40%；约束动态添加/替换时下降超过9%，揭示现有模型在多轮对话中指令遵循能力仍然不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准主要关注单轮或短多轮场景，缺乏对长程多轮对话中指令遵循能力的评估，无法衡量模型在处理用户不断优化、修改或矛盾指令时的表现。

Method: 提出SEQUOR自动基准，基于真实对话中提取的约束，构建模拟人物驱动的多轮交互，评估模型在不同对话长度、不同约束数量以及约束动态变化情况下的指令遵循准确率。

Result: 单约束场景下，随对话增长准确率下降超11%；多约束同时遵循时准确率下降超40%；约束在任意位置添加或替换时准确率下降超9%。

Conclusion: 当前模型在多轮对话中仍难以稳定遵循用户指令，SEQUOR为更好衡量助手指令遵循能力提供了有效评估手段。

Abstract: In a conversation, a helpful assistant must reliably follow user directives, even as they refine, modify, or contradict earlier requests. Yet most instruction-following benchmarks focus on single-turn or short multi-turn scenarios, leaving open how well models handle long-horizon instruction-following tasks. To bridge this gap, we present SEQUOR, an automatic benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in long multi-turn conversations. SEQUOR consists of simulated persona-driven interactions built with constraints extracted from real-world conversations. Our results show that even when following a single constraint, instruction-following accuracy consistently decreases as the conversation grows longer, with drops exceeding 11%. This decline becomes larger when models have to follow multiple constraints simultaneously, reducing their accuracy by over 40%. In scenarios where constraints are added or replaced at arbitrary points of the conversation, model accuracy decreases by more than 9%. Taken together, our results reveal that current models still struggle to follow user instructions in multi-turn conversations, and provide a way for better measuring instruction-following capabilities in assistants.

</details>


### [42] [GATHER: Convergence-Centric Hyper-Entity Retrieval for Zero-Shot Cell-Type Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06403)
*Zhonghui Zhang,Feng Jiang,Shaowei Qin,Jiahao Zhao,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GATHER方法，一种面向超实体查询的收敛中心检索器，通过在细胞知识图谱上进行全局多源遍历并识别拓扑收敛节点，无需LLM参与检索即可实现零样本单细胞类型注释，在免疫和肺数据集上以仅单次LLM调用超越现有KG-RAG基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于知识图谱的RAG方法在零样本单细胞类型注释中面临超实体查询（数十至数百个基因）的挑战：局部实体级探索策略从单个基因出发进行推理，忽视了基因间的协同涌现性，导致可扩展性差且LLM调用成本高昂。

Method: 提出GATHER框架：1）构建细胞中心生物学知识图谱（VCKG）；2）在知识图谱上执行全局多源图遍历，识别从多个输入基因联合可达的拓扑收敛节点；3）结合节点重要性和路径重要性评分，完全无需LLM参与即可筛选信息性证据；4）将收敛节点作为高信息密度超实体证据输入LLM进行单次推理。

Result: 在免疫和肺数据集上，GATHER分别达到27.45%和59.64%的精确匹配准确率，超越ToG、ToG-2、RoG、PoG等强基线方法，且每个样本仅需单次LLM调用（基线方法需2-61次调用）。

Conclusion: 收敛节点能够将多实体信号压缩为紧凑高信息密度的证据，其每个条目携带的信息量超过多跳路径，为局部实体级推理提供了一种高效的全局替代方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot single-cell cell-type annotation aims to determine a cell's type from a given set of expressed genes without any training. Existing knowledge-graph-based RAG approaches retrieve evidence by expanding from source entities and relying on iterative LLM reasoning. However, in this setting each query contains tens to hundreds of genes, where no single gene is decisive and the label emerges only from their collective co-occurrence. Such hyper-entity queries fundamentally challenge local, entity-wise exploration strategies, which reason from individual genes, leading to poor scalability and substantial LLM cost. We propose GATHER (Graph-Aware Traversal with Hyper-Entity Retrieval), a convergence-centric retriever tailored to hyper-entity queries. It performs global multi-source graph traversal and identifies topological convergence points -- nodes jointly reachable from many input genes. These convergence nodes act as high-information hyper-entities that capture entity synergy. By incorporating node- and path-importance scoring, GATHER selects informative evidence entirely without LLM involvement during retrieval. Instantiated on a self-constructed cell-centric biological knowledge graph (VCKG), GATHER outperforms strong KG-RAG baselines (ToG, ToG-2, RoG, PoG) on two datasets (Immune and Lung), achieving the highest exact-match accuracy (27.45% and 59.64%) with only a single LLM call per sample, compared to 2--61 calls for KG-RAG baselines. Our results demonstrate that convergence nodes compress multi-entity signals into compact, high-information evidence that conveys more per item than multi-hop paths, providing an efficient global alternative to local entity-wise reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [MiA-Signature: Approximating Global Activation for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06416)
*Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Mo Yu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 受认知科学中全局点火(global ignition)机制的启发，本文提出心智印记激活签名(MiA-Signature)，一种用于大型语言模型系统的压缩全局激活表征，通过子模选择提取覆盖激活上下文空间的高层概念，并结合工作记忆迭代优化，作为近似完整激活状态的条件信号，在RAG和智能体系统中对多项长上下文理解任务取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学表明可报告的意识访问与分布式记忆系统上的全局点火相关，但个体无法直接访问或枚举所有激活内容，暗示认知可能依赖一种紧凑表征来近似激活对下游处理的全局影响。本文受此启发，旨在为LLM系统设计一种类似的压缩全局激活表征机制。

Method: 引入MiA-Signature概念：通过子模函数(submodular-based selection)从激活上下文空间中选择高层概念，形成压缩表征；可选地通过工作记忆进行轻量级迭代更新来精炼该签名；最终将MiA-Signature作为条件信号注入系统，近似完整激活状态的效果。

Result: 将MiA-Signature集成到RAG和智能体系统中，在多项长上下文理解任务上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的认知启发式压缩表征机制MiA-Signature能够有效近似全局激活状态，在保持计算可行性的同时提升LLM系统的长上下文理解能力，验证了从认知科学中借鉴全局点火机制到AI系统的可行性。

Abstract: A growing body of work in cognitive science suggests that reportable conscious access is associated with \emph{global ignition} over distributed memory systems, while such activation is only partially accessible as individuals cannot directly access or enumerate all activated contents. This tension suggests a plausible mechanism that cognition may rely on a compact representation that approximates the global influence of activation on downstream processing. Inspired by this idea, we introduce the concept of \textbf{Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)}, a compressed representation of the global activation pattern induced by a query. In LLM systems, this is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, optionally refined through lightweight iterative updates using working memory. The resulting MiA-Signature serves as a conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks.

</details>


### [44] [From 124 Million Tokens to 1,021 Neologisms: A Large-Scale Pipeline for Automatic Neologism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06426)
*Diego Rossini,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合规则过滤与LLM分类的可扩展模块化管道，用于自动检测新词。基于语法与非语法形态学框架，从5.27亿条Reddit帖子中提取1,021个候选新词（压缩率超99.99%），经人工验证确认599个（58.7%）为真实词汇创新。揭示了LLM间的显著分类分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语料中自动检测新词面临两大挑战：一是新词定义模糊，缺乏统一理论框架；二是现有方法难以在高召回率下保持精度，且缺乏可迁移的模块化方案。

Method: 采用双阶段流水线：第一阶段基于规则过滤（语法与非语法形态学框架）将1.246亿唯一token压缩至1,021个候选；第二阶段由多个LLM独立分类并通过多数投票整合，辅以人工专家验证。

Result: 从5.27亿Reddit帖子（2005-2024）中提取1.246亿唯一token，经规则过滤减少99.99%以上得到1,021个候选。人工标注确认599个（58.7%）为真正的新词。不同LLM之间存在显著分类分歧。

Conclusion: 模块化流水线可有效缩小新词检测范围，但LLM间的分类不一致性表明该任务在规模化场景下仍具挑战性。代码与标注数据已开源。

Abstract: We present a scalable, modular pipeline for automatic neologism detection that combines rule-based filtering with LLM classification. The pipeline is grounded in two complementary word-formation frameworks, grammatical and extra-grammatical morphology, which jointly define the scope of what counts as a neologism and inform a four-class classification scheme (neologism, entity, foreign, none). While designed to be modular and transferable at the architectural level, the pipeline is instantiated on 527 million English-language Reddit posts spanning 2005-2024. From this corpus, we extract 124.6 million unique tokens and reduce them by over 99.99% to yield 1,021 neologism candidates, a set small enough for manual expert verification. Multiple LLMs independently classify each candidate via majority vote, with a final verification step, revealing substantial cross-model disagreement and highlighting the challenge of operationalizing neologism detection at scale. Manual annotation of all 1,021 candidates confirms that 599 (58.7%) are genuine lexical innovations. The pipeline code, vocabulary compilation scripts, and the annotated candidate list are available at https://github.com/DiegoRossini/neologism-pipeline.

</details>


### [45] [COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435)
*Balakrishnan Vimala,Hii Lee Zing,Laporte Eric*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验验证了文本特征（如词二元组）和语言特征（如词性分布）在传统机器学习方法（随机森林、支持向量机等）中用于假新闻检测的有效性，发现单独使用这些特征能提升检测效果，但组合使用并未显著提升，且随机森林表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有经验证据表明文本和语言特征有助于区分真假新闻，但这些内容特征在假新闻检测中的研究仍不充分，因此该研究旨在系统探究这些特征的实际效用。

Method: 使用COVID-19疫情期间收集的新数据集，选取词二元组和词性分布等文本与语言特征，采用决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机和随机森林五种传统机器学习模型进行实验。

Result: 随机森林在所有设置中表现最佳，支持向量机紧随其后；文本特征和语言特征单独使用时均能提升假新闻检测效果，但组合使用未带来显著提升；二元组与词性标签在检测中表现出差异。

Conclusion: 研究表明，文本和语言特征可成功用于传统机器学习方法的假新闻检测，无需依赖深度学习，且随机森林是最优模型。

Abstract: The use of content features, particularly textual and linguistic for fake news detection is under-researched, despite empirical evidence showing the features could contribute to differentiating real and fake news. To this end, this study investigates a selection of content features such as word bigrams, part of speech distribution etc. to improve fake news detection. We performed a series of experiments on a new dataset gathered during the COVID-19 pandemic and using Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbor, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. Random Forest yielded the best results, followed closely by Support Vector Machine, across all setups. In general, both the textual and linguistic features were found to improve fake news detection when used separately, however, combining them into a single model did not improve the detection significantly. Differences were also noted between the use of bigrams and part of speech tags. The study shows that textual and linguistic features can be used successfully in detecting fake news using the traditional machine learning approach as opposed to deep learning.

</details>


### [46] [Towards Emotion Consistency Analysis of Large Language Models in Emotional Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06476)
*Sneha Oram,Ojaswita Bhushan,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在情感对话背景下对自身生成回应的一致性，发现模型在面对含有错误预设的查询时表现不佳，尤其在中等情绪内容下更容易受到影响，并且注意力从评估性转向生成性，这对其在高风险情感场景中的部署提出了警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs在情感对话中对自身生成回应的一致性，特别是当查询包含错误预设（false presuppositions）时模型的脆弱性。

Method: 将LLM生成的文本作为查询重新输入同一模型，评估其回应一致性。使用三种不同强度的错误预设查询，涵盖极端和中等两种情绪维度。分析了Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT4o-mini和Mistral-7B三个模型，并进行了基于注意力分数的扩展分析。

Result: LLMs在面对含有错误预设的查询时表现低于平均水平，对错误信念的脆弱性显著，中等情绪内容下尤为突出。注意力分析表明模型优先级从评估性转向生成性。

Conclusion: LLMs在情感对话中缺乏对自身生成回应的一致性，容易受到错误预设的影响，这对其在高风险、情感敏感场景中的部署提出了重要考量。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct an analysis to examine the consistency of Large Language Models (LLMs) with respect to their own generated responses in an emotionally-driven conversational context. Specifically, the text generated by LLM is framed as a query to the same model, and its responses are subsequently assessed. This is performed with three queries across two dimensions of extreme and moderate emotions. The three queries are, in particular, false claim queries that contain inherently wrong assumptions (false presuppositions) in increasing order of intensity. Two commercial models, Claude-3.5-haiku, GPT4o-mini, and a medium-sized model, Mistral-7B, are considered in the study. Our findings indicate that LLMs exhibit below-average performance and remain vulnerable to false beliefs embedded within queries. This susceptibility is especially pronounced for moderate emotional content. Furthermore, an extended attention-score-based analysis highlights a shift in models' priority from evaluative to generative. The results raise important considerations for LLMs' deployment in high-stakes, emotionally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [47] [The Frequency Confound in Language-Model Surprisal and Metaphor Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06506)
*Omar Momen,Sina Zarrieß*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，在预测隐喻新颖性方面，词汇频率是比语言模型惊讶度更强的预测因子，且惊讶度与新颖性的关联在训练早期达到峰值后下降，表明此前研究中惊讶度对隐喻新颖性的预测作用可能主要由词汇频率驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型惊讶度（surprisal）与隐喻新颖性判断之间的关系，特别是解耦惊讶度与词汇频率的相互影响，检验惊讶度是否真正反映语境可预测性对隐喻加工的作用。

Method: 使用8种不同规模的Pythia模型和154个训练检查点，分析惊讶度估计值与两种词汇频率指标对隐喻新颖性评分的影响，比较惊讶度和频率的预测强度，并观察不同训练阶段中这些指标的变化规律。

Result: 词汇频率是比惊讶度更强的隐喻新颖性预测因子；惊讶度-新颖性关联在训练早期达到峰值后下降，同时惊讶度-频率关联也呈现类似的时间变化模式。

Conclusion: 以往研究中报告的最佳语言模型惊讶度设置可能错误地将语境可预测性与隐喻新颖性和加工困难度联系起来，而词汇频率可能是主要的潜在影响因素。

Abstract: Language-model (LM) surprisal is widely used as a proxy for contextual predictability and has been reported to correlate with metaphor novelty judgments. However, surprisal is tightly intertwined with lexical frequency. We explore this interaction on metaphor novelty ratings using two different word frequency measures. We analyse surprisal estimates from eight Pythia model sizes and 154 training checkpoints. Across settings, word frequency is a stronger predictor of metaphor novelty than surprisal. Across training stages, the surprisal--novelty association peaks at an early stage and then falls again, mirroring a similarly timed increase in the surprisal--frequency association. These results suggest that the often-reported optimal LM surprisal settings may incorrectly associate contextual predictability with metaphor novelty and processing difficulty, whereas lexical frequency may be the major underlying factor.

</details>


### [48] [Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06546)
*Bowen Peng,Théo Gigant,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Superposition Training (TST)，一种无需修改模型架构、优化器、分词器等组件的即插即用预训练方法。TST包含两个阶段：高效叠加阶段（将多个连续token合并为一袋，使用多热交叉熵目标训练）和恢复阶段（回归标准训练）。在270M、600M、3B和10B A1B MoE模型上验证，TST在同等损失下可将预训练时间减少2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练在扩展时计算效率低下且成本高昂，现有提升数据吞吐量的方法通常需要对并行策略、优化器或模型架构进行复杂且侵入性的修改。

Method: TST分为两阶段：(i) 高效叠加阶段，将多个连续token合并为一个"袋"，使用多热交叉熵(MCE)损失函数进行训练；(ii) 恢复阶段，切换回标准训练方式。该方法不修改并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构。

Result: 在270M、600M参数模型以及3B、10B A1B混合专家模型上均验证了鲁棒性。TST持续优于基线损失和下游评估；在同等损失设定下，10B A1B规模上总预训练时间减少高达2.5倍。

Conclusion: TST是一种简单、即插即用的方法，能显著提升预训练中每FLOPs的数据吞吐量，且不引入复杂修改，在多种规模下表现稳健高效。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [49] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cola DLM是一种分层潜在扩散语言模型，通过层级信息分解（Text VAE + 块因果DiT + 条件解码）实现文本生成，其扩散过程执行潜在先验传输而非令牌级观测恢复，从而分离全局语义组织与局部文本实现，在约2B参数和~2000 EFLOPs的实验中展现出强扩展行为，验证了分层连续潜在先验建模可作为严格令牌级语言建模的原则性替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归范式虽取得显著成功，但高质量文本生成未必需要固定的从左到右顺序；现有替代方案在生成效率、可扩展表示学习和有效全局语义建模方面仍难以兼顾。

Method: 提出Cola DLM，包含三个阶段：(1) 用Text VAE学习稳定的文本到潜在映射；(2) 用块因果DiT在连续潜在空间中建模全局语义先验；(3) 通过条件解码生成文本。扩散过程执行潜在先验传输而非令牌级观测恢复，从统一马尔可夫路径视角分离全局语义组织与局部文本实现。

Result: 在4个研究问题、8个基准、严格匹配的~2B参数自回归和LLaDA基线以及~2000 EFLOPs的扩展曲线上，识别出Cola DLM的有效整体配置，并验证其强大的文本生成扩展行为。

Conclusion: 分层连续潜在先验建模可作为严格令牌级语言建模的原则性替代方案，生成质量和扩展行为可能比似然度更能反映模型能力，同时为跨离散文本和连续模态的统一建模提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [50] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Lighthouse Attention，一种基于对称选择的分层注意力算法，用于训练因果Transformer时替代全量注意力，在训练前期显著降低计算复杂度，训练末期可无缝恢复为全注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练超长序列的因果Transformer受限于缩放点积注意力的二次时间和内存复杂度，亟需一种高效且易于集成的训练加速方案。

Method: (i) 次二次分层预处理/后处理步骤，实现序列的自适应压缩与解压缩；(ii) 对称压缩策略，同时对查询、键、值进行池化并保持从左到右的因果性，提升并行度；(iii) 两阶段训练策略：前期使用Lighthouse Attention预训练，后期短时间微调恢复全注意力模型。该分层选择无梯度，避免了复杂的反向传播内核。

Result: 在小规模LLM预训练实验中，与全量注意力训练相比，在同等设置下实现了更快的总训练时间和更低的最终损失（恢复阶段后）。

Conclusion: Lighthouse Attention作为一种训练专用的对称选择分层注意力算法，能有效加速因果Transformer的训练，同时通过两阶段策略保证最终模型质量不降反升。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>


### [51] [Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06594)
*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无参考报告的远程认知康复场景下，基于规则的模板系统与零样本GPT-4在自动生成临床报告上的对比。模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上得分更高，GPT-4输出更简洁，但两者均未达到统计显著性。基于评估反馈提出八项设计建议，并为低资源临床自然语言生成提供了可复现的方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知康复治疗需求增长与言语治疗师资源有限之间的矛盾，推动了远程康复工具的普及，但这些工具产生的大量交互数据难以被临床医生有效审阅，亟需自动化临床报告生成方法。

Method: 对比两种方法：(1) 基于规则的模板系统，编码言语治疗领域知识作为显式决策规则和验证模板；(2) 零样本GPT-4方法。两者使用相同的预提取、专家验证的结构化变量。由八名言语治疗师和应届毕业生使用九标准问卷进行评估。

Result: 模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现方面得分更高，GPT-4输出更简洁。各维度上方向性差异一致，但经校正后均未达到统计显著性。

Conclusion: 临床可靠性与语言质量之间存在明确权衡。基于评估反馈提炼出八项设计建议，并为低资源临床自然语言生成提供了结合专家启发、分类驱动生成和多维人工评估的可复现方法论，展示了可控比较如何指导生成式AI在医疗中的负责任采用。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [52] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSD统一框架，系统研究自蒸馏在自回归大语言模型中的应用，通过集成多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪等互补机制，揭示各组件的作用与交互规律，最终构建的UniSDfull相比基础模型提升5.4个点，相比最强基线提升2.8个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏在自回归LLM中面临挑战：自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性依赖任务、合理推理仍可能提供不稳定监督。现有方法主要考察孤立设计选择，其有效性、角色和交互关系尚不清晰，需要统一框架进行系统研究。

Method: 提出UniSD统一框架，集成五种互补机制：多教师一致性（Multi-teacher Agreement）、EMA教师稳定化（EMA Teacher Stabilization）、token级对比学习（Token-level Contrastive Learning）、特征匹配（Feature Matching）和散度裁剪（Divergence Clipping），以分别解决监督可靠性、表征对齐和训练稳定性问题。

Result: 在6个基准、6个模型（来自3个模型家族）上验证，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、各组件驱动增益的机制及跨任务交互规律。构建的UniSDfull集成管道相比基础模型提升+5.4点，相比最强基线提升+2.8点。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏是一种实用且可操控的高效LLM适配方法，无需依赖更强外部教师即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [53] [Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06619)
*Jan Fillies,Ronald E. Robertson,Jeffrey Hancock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文形式化了Algospeak（算法语言）中逃避检测与可理解性之间的动态权衡，提出“多数可理解调制”（MUM）概念，并基于COVID-19虚假信息构建了700条可调制的参考数据集，通过七种语言模型验证了调制水平与可检测性、可理解性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在内容生成与审核中的广泛应用，使得Algospeak这类语言规避策略加速了规避者与检测器之间的共同进化。现有研究缺乏对这种权衡关系的形式化描述和可复现的实验框架。

Method: 基于联合行动模型形式化Algospeak的动态关系；提出MUM概念；基于现有分类法构建含五种调制水平和七种策略的可复现框架；在COVID-19虚假信息场景下构建700条参考数据集；通过七种语言模型进行意义恢复（理解性）和虚假信息检测（可检测性）两项评估；对调制水平进行曲线拟合以估计MUM阈值。

Result: 得到了可理解性与调制水平之间的特征关系曲线，估计出多数可理解调制（MUM）阈值，并实现了跨策略和跨模型的敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解Algospeak背后的动态机制奠定了基础，提供了可复现的框架、数据集和实验设置，有助于未来在内容审核与语言生成领域更好地应对语言规避策略。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [54] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [55] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StraTA框架，通过在智能体强化学习中引入显式的轨迹级策略抽象，采用分层GRPO式回滚设计和多样化策略展开与自我批判，显著提升了LLM在长时域决策任务中的样本效率和最终性能，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法多为纯反应式，在长时域决策中面临探索不足和信用分配困难的问题，亟需一种能引入高层策略引导来改善长轨迹优化的方法。

Method: StraTA框架：从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，使后续动作条件于该策略；采用分层GRPO式回滚设计联合训练策略生成与动作执行；通过多样化策略展开和关键自我评判增强训练效果。

Result: ALFWorld成功率93.1%，WebShop成功率84.2%，SciWorld总体得分63.5%，超越前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: 引入显式轨迹级策略抽象能有效提升LLM智能体在长时域决策中的样本效率和性能，StraTA框架简单有效且具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [56] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO 是一种通过文档级专家共享约束实现模块化的 MoE 架构，在保持全模型性能的同时，支持仅使用 25% 专家即可达到接近完整模型的效果，且专家子集在语义层面（如数学、代码）产生专业化分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常作为 monolithic 系统部署，即使应用只需要狭窄能力子集（如代码、数学）也需加载完整模型。标准 MoE 虽然在理论上可激活部分专家，但实践中子集推理会导致严重性能下降，限制了其在内存受限场景下的实用性。

Method: 提出 EMO，核心思想是鼓励相同领域的 token 依赖相似的专家。利用文档边界作为先验，约束同一文档内的 token 从共享的专家池中选择专家，而不同文档可使用不同专家池。这种简单的约束使得在预训练中自动形成连贯的专家分组。

Result: 预训练了 1B 活跃/14B 总参数的 EMO 模型（1T token）。全模型性能与标准 MoE 持平。选择性使用 25%（12.5%）的专家时，仅下降 1%（3%）绝对性能，而标准 MoE 在此设置下完全失效。专家子集在语义层面（如数学、代码）产生专业化，而非标准 MoE 的低级句法专业化。

Conclusion: EMO 展示了实现模块化、内存高效部署大规模稀疏模型的路径，为可组合架构开辟了新机会。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>

### [57] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向电子健康记录数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，基于两年诊断前历史预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS），整体AUC达到0.8461，比最佳基线提升0.0168。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见且异质性高的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且表型异质性掩盖了风险模式。既往预测研究多依赖单机构队列，缺乏人群层面的泛化性。

Method: 利用All of Us研究计划的全国性纵向EHR数据，基于两年诊断前历史预测CRS。采用混合特征选择管道（基于患病率的统计筛选+基于模型的重要性排序），将约110,000个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征。针对六组成年性别和生命阶段亚组训练人口统计学分层模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达0.8461，比最佳基线提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可以支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，为初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先排序提供依据。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [58] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），一种无协调器的训练范式，通过分块坐标更新和逐智能体KL信任域，使多个小模型组成的团队在无需中心控制器的情况下协作训练，并保证单调改进和即插即用不变性。三个4B智能体团队（总计12B）在AIME24/25基准上超越Qwen3-32B，平均提升3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）虽性能强大但部署成本高昂。近期工作探索使用多个较小、更高效的LLM组成的团队来匹配甚至超越单个大模型，但联合更新多个智能体会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练过程中协调困难且不稳定。

Method: 提出Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT)，一种无协调器的训练范式。将团队表示为因子化策略，采用分块坐标更新方式逐智能体更新，无需中心控制器。具体包含：（1）一种序列感知的、基于当前团队策略的on-policy优势估计器；（2）逐智能体KL信任域，隔离占据漂移。理论上保证单调改进和可证明的即插即用不变性。

Result: 三个4B智能体团队（总计12B）在AIME24/25基准上超越Qwen3-32B，平均提升3.9%。通过替换两个8B智能体验证了即插即用理论，复合分数提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT提供了一种可扩展、去中心化的多智能体训练范式，具有单调改进保证和即插即用不变性，允许单个智能体升级为更强模型而无需重新训练整个团队，有效降低部署成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [59] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**horizon-constrained Rashomon sets**框架，首次严格建立了混沌动力学与预测多重性之间的理论联系。研究发现混沌系统中Rashomon集随预测时间跨度以最大Lyapunov指数速率指数收缩，并据此设计了基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法。在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成系统及风电、交通、天气等真实场景中，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）与混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基础挑战，尽管它们之间存在概念上的内在联系。现有研究缺乏在混沌系统中理解模型多重性如何随预测时间演化的理论框架，导致在安全关键领域部署机器学习模型时缺乏原则性指导。

Method: 提出horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，刻画混沌系统中Rashomon集随预测时间跨度的演化规律；证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率指数收缩；引入Lyapunov加权度量提供预测分歧的更紧界；开发基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统和风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，所提框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格连接，为在安全关键混沌领域中部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [60] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了概念引导（Concept Steering）的理论框架，将标准引导方法与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）联系起来，发展了概念切换理论（LEACE-Switch），并在此基础上提出了更通用的MidSteer框架，实现最小干扰的概念操控，在多种模型和任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管概念引导在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 建立了引导与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；2) 提出了LEACE-Switch框架，刻画了概念切换的最优仿射解条件；3) 引入MidSteer（最小干扰概念引导），一个更通用的仿射框架，放宽了假设条件，实现定向的最小干扰变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文为概念引导提供了系统的理论形式化，并提出了更具通用性和有效性的MidSteer方法。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [61] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种在相邻Transformer块之间添加轻量级门控机制的方法，为每个token独立决定是否跳过残差更新，实现端到端可微的差异化深度分配，在字符级语言建模上以极低质量损失节省14-23%计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同层数，未考虑不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 在相邻Transformer块之间插入一个轻量级两层MLP作为门控，为每个token生成连续的停止概率（残差更新门控），使得整个机制端到端可微，仅增加1.7%的参数开销，且无需修改基础架构。

Result: 即使在没有深度正则化的情况下（λ=0），仅靠任务损失梯度驱动，门控机制也能跳过20%的token-层操作。在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上，以<0.5%的质量损失节省14-23%的TLOps。在相同效率下，验证损失比early exit低0.7%。

Conclusion: TSA能够学习与难度成正比的差异化路由策略，训练后的门控可直接用于推理时的稀疏执行，实现真实的加速效果。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [62] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架分析稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征组合不稳定性，推导出高维稀疏锥流形上的组合坍塌阈值，揭示ReLU整流在组合中产生类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，并在CLEVR数据集上验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE在特征组合激活（compositional steering）时存在非线性干扰，但理论基础不足；线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中的非线性效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，利用球面字典模型和高斯平均宽度（统计维数）推导组合坍塌阈值；分析高偏置下ReLU整流将相关诱导方差波动转化为系统性漂移的棘轮效应。

Result: 在CLEVR语义特征上验证了理论预测的缩放趋势，层级相关加速了从随机基线到坍塌的过渡。

Conclusion: 组合激活的可扩展性受几何约束限制，需设计显式管理干扰的组合机制，超越朴素线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [63] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的行为，发现冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UEs方法的效果，并提出了浅层语义伪装(SSC)分层欺骗策略来应对这一挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要关注从头训练场景，而忽略了在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的表现，存在关键空白。

Method: 通过语义过滤机制分析发现PF范式下冻结的浅层保留了语义信息从而过滤了不可学习噪声；提出SSC方法，将对抗扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间以绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性场景下持续保持数据不可学习性，弥补了预训练范式下的关键差距。

Conclusion: 预训练-微调范式会削弱现有UEs的效果，而本文提出的SSC策略能有效应对该问题，为实际场景下的数据隐私保护提供了新方案。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [64] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS是一个无需训练的高效推理框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中因信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中面临严重的效率瓶颈（落后节点效应），现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法处理多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源分配不当。

Method: MACS提出两个核心组件：(1) 熵加权负载机制——通过计算视觉token的熵值量化其语义价值，解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制——根据输入实时的模态组成（视觉与文本比例）动态分配专家资源。整个框架无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试上，MACS显著优于现有方法，为MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: MACS通过无需训练的轻量级方法有效解决了多模态场景下MoE模型的负载均衡问题，兼顾了视觉token的语义差异和模态比例动态变化，实现了推理效率的显著提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [65] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种监督内化方法，将强化学习中的结果级监督自动转化为过程级学习信号，通过识别、修正和复用失败推理轨迹实现更细粒度的策略优化，并抽象为一种新的训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法要么依赖结果级奖励导致信用分配困难，要么依赖外部构建的过程监督成本高且难以持续扩展，亟需一种能在仅结果监督下自动生成细粒度学习信号的方法。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，通过使模型自动识别、修正和复用失败的推理轨迹，从结果监督中提取过程级学习信号；并进一步抽象为一种新的训练范式，使模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并精炼自身的过程监督。

Result: 实现了在仅结果监督条件下获得比肩外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配效果，验证了监督内化方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 强化学习中的推理可理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题，所提方法开辟了一条不同于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配新路径。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [66] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种在线数据重加权框架，通过自适应每样本学习率来动态调整训练样本重要性，替代了传统的离线数据筛选和混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选和混合方法采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、面对模型/任务迁移时需要重新运行整个流程，且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模会牺牲多样性并损害泛化能力。

Method: ADAPT将数据筛选重新定义为在线重加权问题，通过基于相似性质量信号的自适应每样本学习率动态调整训练样本重要性，不改变样本数量，实现隐式课程学习。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在相同FLOPs下持续优于离线筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权是比离线数据筛选更优的数据策展范式，ADAPT通过自适应学习率机制有效提升了LLM训练的泛化性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [67] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略优化预训练量化模型的量化状态分配，通过迭代调整少量权重的量化值来提升模型精度，在VGG、ResNet等架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备、实时系统等场景中因复杂度和内存过大而受限，量化是一种主流压缩技术，但基于最近邻的量化方法无法保证最优精度，亟需提升量化效率。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化方法，每次迭代仅改变少量权重的值，将其转移到不同的量化状态，通过合适的算子与参数设置快速提升量化模型精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet等图像分类与检测架构以及自动编码器架构上验证了所提方法能够快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 进化策略配合适当的算子与参数可有效改善预训练量化模型的精度，克服最近邻量化非最优的问题，为模型压缩提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [68] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计每个Transformer层中参数的任务重要性分数来调节梯度，缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在持续学习中存在灾难性遗忘问题，现有方法（数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对模型内部分知识分布的语义感知，无法区分哪些参数应保留或更新。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架：估计每个Transformer层中任务的逐元素参数重要性分数，利用这些分数调节梯度——对先前任务重要的参数更新幅度小，不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准上，该方法一致优于基线方法，在保持旧任务性能的同时，在新任务上具有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 归因引导的参数重要性估计能有效区分关键参数和非关键参数，通过梯度调制实现更好的旧任务保留和新任务学习平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [69] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CopyCop算法，能够在不同架构、权重和嵌入维度条件下，检测出未经授权的模仿GNN（复制猫模型），并提供理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印和指纹方法无法在攻击者变换嵌入的情况下识别出模仿另一个GNN的复制猫模型，需要一种更鲁棒的检测方案。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，通过分析两个GNN输出的节点嵌入之间的关系，判断它们是否独立训练，而非一个刻意模仿另一个。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且鲁棒，能抵抗多种对抗攻击和变换。

Conclusion: CopyCop能够有效识别复制猫GNN，弥补了现有方法的不足，并有理论保证。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [70] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（<5%），且需要针对无先验数据的新型蛋白质高效筛选高质量配体，现有方法在样本效率和计算速度上存在不足。

Method: SPADE提出了一种新颖的配体选择方法，通过从稀疏数据中学习来进行高效迭代筛选，具体算法细节需查阅原文。

Result: 平均40次测试找到10个高质量配体；在逐对比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；计算速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为无先验数据的新型蛋白质药物发现提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [71] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO），针对数据分布偏移下多目标优化问题，设计了两类多梯度下降算法，实现了帕累托平稳点的样本复杂度从𝒪(ε⁻¹²)降至𝒪(ε⁻⁴)，并在非凸设定下提供理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法未显式考虑数据分布偏移，这在分布外泛化场景下会导致优化结果不可靠。

Method: 提出DR-MOO框架，将每个目标在各自最坏情况分布下优化；采用拉格朗日对偶重构，设计双环MGDA（内环估计对偶变量，样本复杂度𝒪(ε⁻¹²)）和单环双裁剪MGDA（利用梯度裁剪处理广义光滑和有偏梯度，样本复杂度𝒪(ε⁻⁴)）。

Result: 双环MGDA达到𝒪(ε⁻¹²)样本复杂度，单环双裁剪MGDA提升至𝒪(ε⁻⁴)，且无需有界目标或梯度假设。

Conclusion: DR-MOO为分布偏移下的多目标优化提供了理论基础和高效算法，实验表明与现有MGDA基线方法竞争力强。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data. We introduce distributionally robust multi-objective optimization (DR-MOO), which minimizes multiple objectives under their respective worst-case distributions. We propose Pareto-type solution concepts for DR-MOO and develop multi-gradient descent algorithms (MGDA) with provable guarantees. Leveraging a Lagrangian dual reformulation, we first design a double-loop MGDA that uses an inner loop to estimate dual variables and achieves a total sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$ for reaching an $ε$-Pareto-stationary point. To further improve efficiency, we incorporate gradient clipping to handle generalized-smooth and biased gradient estimates, removing the need for double sampling. This yields a single-loop double-clip MGDA with substantially improved sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$. Our theory applies to the nonconvex setting and does not require bounded objectives or gradients. Experiments demonstrate that our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art MGDA baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于预训练扩散模型的零样本条件采样，针对线性逆问题（如图像修复、超分辨率）。通过分数函数的法向-切向分解，证明无条件切向分数替代真实条件切向分数的误差受条件互信息上界约束，并提出投影-朗之万初始化结合引导反向去噪的方法，在实验中优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投影的条件采样方法仅强制测量一致性，但无法正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件采样存在偏差。本文旨在从理论角度分析该偏差来源，并提出修正方法。

Method: 对分数函数进行法向-切向分解，其中观测方向分数由测量精确确定，仅切向条件分数未知。证明用无条件切向分数替代的误差受维度无关的条件互信息上界约束，并据此提出投影-朗之万初始化+引导反向去噪的框架。

Result: 理论证明了误差上界，实验表明所提方法在图像修复和超分辨率任务中优于基于投影的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 条件采样偏差可归因于初始化误差和路径分数失配误差，所提出的信息论分解和方法有效提升了零样本条件采样的质量。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [73] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，通过预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，有效解决了多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性瓶颈，在6种VRP变体上实现了与SOTA相当或更优的解质量，同时显著提升了训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图场景（平行边代表不同旅行选项）缺乏高效的可扩展方法，已有方法面临严重的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略拆分为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段，以及分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在6种VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上达到或超越SOTA，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF有效解决了多图VRP问题的可扩展性挑战，为处理具有多种旅行选项的复杂路由问题提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [74] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含事件驱动的半显式微分代数方程系统的可微参数优化，提出了两种互补的梯度计算方法：自动微分-through-仿真方法和显式离散伴随方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含状态事件的微分代数方程系统在工程中广泛存在（如混合系统、接触动力学等），但其梯度学习面临隐式代数变量、参数依赖事件时间、重置映射不连续等挑战，尚无系统的梯度计算方法。

Method: 将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题；提出两种梯度策略：(1) AD-through-simulation：用隐函数定理微分代数求解，分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随方法：将前向仿真表示为事件分割残差系统，通过求解拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向仿真选定的事件路径提供梯度，在固定事件顺序和横截守卫穿越条件下有效；阐明了伴随方法中残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Conclusion: 系统比较了两方法在梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面的差异，为含事件DAE系统的可微编程提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [75] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对核化赌博机优化中的误规范问题，提出通过谱局部化和域分割技术，将误规范项的放大因子从核有效维度的平方根或信息增益的平方根降低到对数或多项式对数增长，显著改进了离线简单遗憾和在线累积遗憾的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核化赌博机优化算法在处理模型误规范时，误规范惩罚项受核复杂度（有效维度或最大信息增益）的放大，导致在实际应用中性能不佳。本文旨在降低这种误规范放大效应。

Method: 在离线设定中，利用谱Lebesgue常数推导高概率简单遗憾界；在在线设定中，修改域分割算法并在局部化特征衰减假设下证明累积遗憾界。核心思想是局部化：谱局部化控制离线近似算子的Lebesgue常数，域分割实现在线设定中的空间局部化机制。

Result: 离线：对一维单调谱得到对数放大，对多维傅里叶对角乘积核得到多项式对数放大。在线：在温和局部特征衰减假设下，累积遗憾界为$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$，移除了误规范项上的额外$\sqrt{γ_n}$因子。
