We instead ask whether the in-group divergence between the partial trajectories at an intermediate step can already predict that the group will be zero-variance: when the parallel rollouts have already converged on the same action prefix, the group is on track to produce a single reward, and we can stop early. We propose a one-parameter gate that stops a group when the mean pairwise prefix edit distance between its partial action sequences falls below a threshold. On a 60-iteration on-policy GRPO run on ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-7B, averaged over four random seeds, the gated arm finishes 10.7% faster in wall-clock (bootstrap 95% CI excludes 0) and shifts held-out success rate on 50 unseen tasks by +2.5 pp, with the held-out gain tracing to a measurable reduction in zero-advantage gradient-batch dilution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/selective-rollout.

</details>


### [112] [Retrieval from Within: An Intrinsic Capability of Attention-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05806)
*Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出INTRA框架，通过注意力机制让编码器-解码器模型直接从自身内部表示中检索证据，统一了检索与生成过程，消除了传统RAG中检索器与生成器的分离和失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG将检索和生成视为分离的系统，存在检索器与生成器之间的失配问题，且需要额外的检索模块和流水线设计，增加了系统复杂性和计算开销。

Method: INTRA框架利用解码器注意力查询对预编码的证据片段进行评分，然后将这些证据直接复用为生成的上下文。通过设计，检索和生成在同一个注意力机制中完成，并复用预计算的编码器状态来分摊编码成本。

Result: 在问答基准测试中，INTRA在证据召回率和端到端答案质量上均优于强工程化的检索流水线。

Conclusion: 注意力模型本身已经具备可以被激发的检索机制，无需作为外部模块额外添加，通过INTRA可以有效利用这一内在能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically treats retrieval and generation as separate systems. We ask whether an attention-based encoder-decoder can instead retrieve directly from its own internal representations. We introduce INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention), a framework where decoder attention queries score pre-encoded evidence chunks that are then directly reused as context for generation. By construction, INTRA unifies retrieval and generation, eliminating the retriever-generator mismatch typical of RAG pipelines. This design also amortizes context encoding by reusing precomputed encoder states across queries. On question-answering benchmarks, INTRA outperforms strong engineered retrieval pipelines on both evidence recall and end-to-end answer quality. Our results demonstrate that attention-based models already possess a retrieval mechanism that can be elicited, rather than added as an external module.

</details>


### [113] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可量化的后验坍塌判定标准：当编码器产生的潜在变量与输入存在可检测的依赖关系，且其对齐损失低于数据集平均教师分布与真实教师分布之间的互信息时，即可证明潜在编码未退化为输入无关的常数。该标准在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200上得到验证，并能检测出标准VAE基线中的坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验坍塌诊断依赖KL项大小、解码器强度等间接症状，缺乏明确的坍塌边界定义。作者希望将输入无关的常数坍塌这一具体失败模式转化为可量化的、有理论保证的判定标准。

Method: （1）理论推导：证明对于任意固定的非恒定教师分布T(·|x)，最优常数学生为数据集平均教师分布，对齐成本为教师互信息I_T(X;T)。（2）构建原始证据（raw witness），若其对齐损失低于该互信息值且存在安全裕度，则证明潜在编码非输入无关常数。（3）在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200上进行实验验证。

Result: （1）在CIFAR-100上，完整训练保持在认证边界的安全侧；移除对齐使原始证据进入常数学生区域；从坍塌检查点重启并启用对齐可恢复认证。（2）Tiny-ImageNet-200上展示了相同的预防-坍塌-恢复模式。（3）标准VAE基线（包括保持重建质量或事后可预测性的方法）在原始认证下均呈阴性。

Conclusion: 该认证标准是窄但严格的：它能证明匹配的非恒定教师相对变异通过了潜在路径，而非排除所有形式的后验坍塌。这为检测和防止特定类型的后验坍塌提供了理论基础和可操作工具。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [114] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过动量优化和分块并行算法改进线性注意力模型，在400M和1.3B参数规模上超越Transformer、Mamba2等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归解释为闭式在线随机梯度下降，但朴素SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和次优收敛问题。动量优化器虽能弥补，但在训练效率和效果之间难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 提出分块并行算法，通过几何重排更新系数实现逐步动量规则；2) 从动力系统视角分析动量递归为二阶系统，引入复共轭特征值，据此设计稳定门控约束；3) 基于Triton内核实现高效训练。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数模型上，MDN在多项下游评估基准中持续优于Transformer、Mamba2、GDN等强基线，且训练吞吐量与Mamba2、KDA等竞争模型相当。

Conclusion: 动量机制有效缓解了线性注意力中SGD更新的信息衰减问题，二阶系统分析指导了门控稳定性设计，MDN在长序列LLM扩展中兼具效率和性能优势。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [115] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数字游戏研究LLM的假设推断能力，发现LLM的行为可用带偏置的双参数贝叶斯模型描述：默认存在强采样假设（隐含奥卡姆剃刀，偏向更窄假设），思考模式则更依赖先验；存在评估-生成鸿沟（评估时选对假设但生成时偏向简单规则）；且贝叶斯偏置模式无法外推，模型对未见假设域泛化差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM如何从部分描述中生成假设并随新信息更新，以及这一推断过程与最优推断（贝叶斯）的接近程度。

Method: 在数字游戏（从正整数集推断规则，如2的幂或近似20的区间）中，使用三种互补探针（后验预测、假设评估、假设生成）测量LLM的后验分布，并与最优贝叶斯模型和人类行为对比，检验跨探针的一致性和泛化能力。

Result: LLM行为可用双参数贝叶斯拟合描述但存在系统性偏移：默认隐含强采样假设（窄假设优先），思考模式增加先验依赖；存在评估-生成鸿沟（评估优于生成）；贝叶斯偏置模式不外推，可观察到的规则性假设在未覆盖域泛化差。

Conclusion: LLM作为通用问题解决者存在局限，尤其对科学推断这类需要超越数据生成假设的任务表现不佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [116] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度-动量耦合（GMC）信号，通过衡量当前样本梯度与历史梯度动量的逐参数归一化绝对乘积，量化样本对持续学习的贡献度，在好奇心驱动探索中替代预测误差，提升对观测噪声的鲁棒性并实现自发课程学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动探索中广泛使用的预测误差信号无法区分有意义的可学习模式与随机噪声，导致智能体容易被噪声干扰而无法有效探索。

Method: 定义GMC信号为当前样本梯度与历史动量之间的逐参数归一化绝对乘积，利用动量对噪声和振荡的自然滤波特性，衡量每个样本梯度对持续参数更新的贡献程度。

Result: 控制实验表明GMC具有噪声鲁棒性并能产生自发课程学习，优先选择学习速度快的任务而非难度大的任务；在MiniGrid环境中，用GMC替代预测误差可提升现有好奇心驱动架构对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于优化动态的GMC信号为好奇心驱动探索提供了一种更可靠的进度度量方式，有望在噪声环境中替代传统的预测误差信号。

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

</details>


### [117] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用离线强化学习和基于模型的离线强化学习算法控制托卡马克等离子体旋转剖面，仅利用DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据训练策略，并在真实装置上部署取得了有希望的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克中控制等离子体旋转剖面是一个重要但尚未解决的挑战，该问题具有高维度、多执行器响应和依赖等离子体条件的特性。传统控制方法难以有效处理，而基于学习的控制方法虽有潜力，但缺乏精确的旋转剖面动力学模拟器来训练策略。

Method: 采用离线RL和离线基于模型的RL算法，仅使用DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据进行训练。最终方法利用等离子体动力学的概率模型生成轨迹用于RL训练，从而在不需要精确模拟器的情况下学习控制策略。

Result: 在DIII-D托卡马克上部署了该策略，并观察到了有希望的真实世界实验结果。

Conclusion: 总结了在仅使用有限历史数据的情况下训练和部署RL策略于复杂物理装置上的关键挑战与洞见。

Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

</details>


### [118] [Do Neural Operators Forget Geometry? The Forgetting Hypothesis in Deep Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05862)
*Yanming Xia,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经算子在非规则几何域上性能受限的根本原因——深度算子架构存在与深度相关的几何遗忘现象，并提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在结构化域上表现良好，但在不规则几何域上的行为尚不明确。现有工作多将其归因于编码问题，本文证明这本质上是深度算子架构固有的深度维故障模式。

Method: 1) 形式化定义几何遗忘假说：算子层的马尔可夫结构及其对全局混合机制的依赖导致深度增加时域几何信息逐步丢失；2) 通过逐层几何探针实验验证该假说在谱算子和注意力算子中的普遍性；3) 提出轻量级几何记忆注入机制，在中间深度恢复几何约束。

Result: 几何遗忘会降低精度、稳定性和泛化能力；所提几何记忆注入机制能以极小架构开销持续缓解遗忘；同时发现基于Transformer的算子存在几何捷径不稳定性，表明几何保持是结构性需求而非设计选择。

Conclusion: 几何遗忘是神经算子深度架构的结构性缺陷，几何记忆注入是一种有效且轻量的缓解手段。几何保持应被视为算子设计的核心结构性需求。

Abstract: Neural operators perform well on structured domains, yet their behaviour on irregular geometries remains poorly understood. We show that this limitation is not merely an encoding issue, but a depth-wise failure mode inherent to deep operator architectures.
  We formalise the Geometric Forgetting Hypothesis: due to the Markovian structure of operator layers and their reliance on global mixing mechanisms, neural operators progressively lose access to domain geometry as depth increases. Using layer-wise geometric probing, we demonstrate that both spectral and attention-based operators systematically lose geometric fidelity.
  We show that this geometric forgetting degrades accuracy, stability, and generalisation. To counteract it, we introduce a lightweight geometry memory injection mechanism that restores geometric constraints at intermediate depths with minimal architectural overhead. This simple intervention consistently mitigates forgetting and exposes a geometric shortcut instability in transformer-based operators, revealing that geometric retention is a structural requirement rather than a design choice.

</details>


### [119] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOPE算法，利用与演员对齐的离策略策略评估信号作为自动早停机制，动态控制离线训练阶段长度，在25个连续控制任务上提升高达45.6%性能并减少22倍计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将先验数据融入在线强化学习时面临高计算成本与多阶段训练管线的权衡，固定长度的稳定化阶段虽计算高效但需要任务相关的手动调参，可能导致先验知识浪费或严重过拟合。

Method: 提出SOPE算法，使用与演员对齐的离策略策略评估（OPE）信号作为自动早停机制，通过在当前策略的动作分布下在留出验证集上评估评论家，在分布外收益饱和时精确停止梯度更新。

Result: 在Minari基准套件的25个连续控制任务上，SOPE将基线性能提升高达45.6%，同时将所需TFLOPs减少高达22倍。

Conclusion: 自适应、评估驱动的更新调度比依赖静态、穷举更新调度更有效，能够平衡样本效率与计算效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


### [120] [QuadraSHAP: Stable and Scalable Shapley Values for Product Games via Gauss-Legendre Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05870)
*Majid Mohammadi,Grigory Reznikov,Pavel Sinitcyn,Krikamol Muandet,Siu Lun Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对乘积博弈（产品游戏）中Shapley值的高效计算方法QuadraSHAP，利用一维积分表示将指数级特征组合求和转化为多项式积分，并采用Gauss-Legendre求积实现精确或高精度近似，同时给出了O(d m_q)工作量和O(log d)并行时间的高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积博弈广泛存在于机器学习可解释性中（如核方法、树模型），但其Shapley值的精确计算通常面临指数复杂度挑战，现有方法在效率和数值稳定性上存在不足。

Method: (1) 推导出乘积博弈中Shapley值的精确一维积分表示，将指数级特征子集加权求和转化为[0,1]上d-1次多项式的积分；(2) 采用Gauss-Legendre求积公式，当节点数m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时精确成立，否则误差呈几何级数衰减；(3) 基于对数空间评估实现数值稳定计算；(4) 利用关联扫描原语实现O(d m_q)工作量和O(log d)并行时间的并行算法。

Result: QuadraSHAP在实验中被验证为所有测试配置中最快的数值稳定方法，少量节点（几百个）即可在数千特征上实现高精度估计。

Conclusion: 乘积博弈的Shapley值计算可转化为多项式积分问题，通过Gauss-Legendre求积实现了理论上可证明的精确/近似保证和实际中的高效并行计算，为可解释性中的Shapley值计算提供了新的高效方案。

Abstract: We study the efficient computation of Shapley values for \emph{product games} -- cooperative games in which the coalition value factorizes as a product of per-player terms. Such games arise in machine learning explainability whenever the value function inherits a multiplicative structure from the underlying model, as in kernel methods with product kernels and tree-based models. Our key result is that the Shapley value of each player in a product game admits an exact one-dimensional integral representation: the weighted sum over exponentially many feature coalitions collapses to the integral of a degree-$(d-1)$ polynomial over $[0,1]$, where $d$ is the total number of features. This yields a Gauss--Legendre quadrature scheme that is \emph{provably exact} whenever the number of nodes satisfies $m_q \geq \lceil d/2 \rceil$, and otherwise provides a \emph{near-exact} approximation with error provably decaying geometrically in $m_q$. In practice, a few hundred nodes can achieve highly precise estimates even with thousands of features. Building on this formulation, we derive a numerically stable implementation via log-space evaluation, together with an efficient parallel implementation based on associative scan primitives that achieves $O(d\,m_q)$ total work and $O(\log d)$ parallel time. Experiments show that \textsc{QuadraSHAP} is the fastest numerically stable method across all tested configurations.

</details>


### [121] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近策略蒸馏（NPD），一种用于自回归模型的异步知识蒸馏框架。NPD将学生模型生成与训练解耦，允许使用序列打包进行监督微调。为解决异步更新导致的策略滞后和样本噪声，NPD引入稀疏学生更新和Δ-IFD过滤机制，在保持高效的同时稳定优化轨迹。实验表明NPD比在线策略基线提速8.1倍，比SFT提升8.09%，并帮助小模型超越大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏用于自回归模型时存在分布不匹配问题；在线策略方法虽能缓解但依赖计算昂贵的强化学习框架。现有方法在效率与效果之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出近策略蒸馏（NPD）异步框架：1）将学生生成与训练解耦，使监督微调可用序列打包；2）引入稀疏学生更新减少计算开销；3）设计Δ-IFD启发式样本选择机制，过滤极端分布外样本，防止噪声主导梯度，确保更新保持在安全近策略学习区域。

Result: NPD框架相比在线策略基线实现8.1倍加速，相比SFT提升8.09%。openPangu-Embedded-1B模型达到68.73%的SOTA得分，超越更大的Qwen3-1.7B模型。

Conclusion: 通过有效缩小后续RL的探索空间，NPD使小模型在高效训练下取得超越大模型的性能，验证了异步近策略蒸馏在自回归模型知识蒸馏中的有效性。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [122] [Retain-Neutral Surrogates for Min-Max Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05871)
*Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Dowon Kim,Sung Il Choi,Chengjun Jin,Juhyun Park,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSU，一种通过保留正交约束的替代点构建方法实现机器去学习，在不损害保留集性能的前提下有效移除指定数据的影响。方法利用闭式保留正交扰动和沿保留中性方向的放大更新，在梯度高度耦合时效果显著，并在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器去学习需要移除指定训练数据的影响，同时保持剩余数据上的性能。近似去学习可视为局部编辑问题，在最小-最大去学习中，当遗忘梯度和保留梯度高度对齐时，无约束的遗忘最大化扰动可能移动到增加保留损失的替代点，导致保留性能下降。

Method: 提出ROSU，约束内层替代点构造，在固定扰动预算下最大化一阶遗忘增益，同时保证一阶保留变化为零。由此导出闭式保留正交扰动、轻量级传输外更新以及沿保留中性方向的放大更新。

Result: 理论分析建立了曲率控制的二阶保留损伤界、正对齐机制下ROSU严格降低替代保留损失的结论，以及梯度近正交时的近似等价性。在CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet、TOFU、WMDP等视觉和语言基准上，ROSU在高耦合场景下增益最明显，其他场景保持竞争力。

Conclusion: ROSU通过保留正交约束有效解决了梯度对齐时的保留损失增加问题，其性能优势与梯度耦合程度密切相关，为近似去学习提供了一种几何驱动的轻量级方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving performance on the remaining data. Approximate unlearning can be viewed as a local editing problem; in min-max unlearning, the key local object is the surrogate point at which the retain objective is evaluated. When forget and retain gradients are strongly aligned, an unconstrained forget-maximizing perturbation can move to a surrogate point that increases retain loss. We propose Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU), which constrains the inner surrogate construction by maximizing first-order forget gain subject to zero first-order retain change under a fixed perturbation budget. This yields a closed-form retain-orthogonal perturbation, a lightweight transported outer update, and amplification along the retain-neutral direction. Our analysis establishes (i) a curvature-controlled second-order bound on retain damage, (ii) a positive-alignment regime in which ROSU strictly reduces surrogate retain loss relative to standard min-max perturbations, and (iii) near-equivalence when the two gradients are nearly orthogonal. Across vision and language benchmarks (CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet, TOFU, WMDP), the empirical pattern follows this geometry: ROSU gives its clearest gains in high-coupling regimes while remaining competitive elsewhere.

</details>


### [123] [RepFlow: Representation Enhanced Flow Matching for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05890)
*Yifei Xie,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RepFlow是一个结合表示学习与条件流匹配的因果效应估计框架，通过最小化熵正则化Wasserstein距离缓解选择偏差，并引入L2归一化约束增强数值稳定性，在点估计和分布估计上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法大多局限于点估计，缺乏分布建模能力，且面临反事实缺失和选择偏差的根本挑战。

Method: 将因果效应估计建模为联合优化问题，集成表示学习与条件流匹配（CFM）；通过最小化熵正则化Wasserstein距离缓解选择偏差；引入L2归一化约束增强数值稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，RepFlow在点估计和分布因果效应估计上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RepFlow有效结合了表示学习与流匹配模型，能够准确捕捉潜在结果的分布，为因果效应估计提供了兼具点估计和分布建模能力的统一框架。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data has become increasingly critical in diverse fields including healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge in causal inference arises from the missing counterfactuals and the selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling. In this work, we propose RepFlow, a novel framework that formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM).
  RepFlow mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations.
  To enhance numerical stability, we further introduce an $L_2$ normalization constraint on latent representations.
  This balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [124] [VisMMOE: Exploiting Visual-Expert Affinity for Efficient Visual-Language MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05899)
*Cheng Xu,Xiaofeng Hou,Jiacheng Liu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VisMMoE是一种面向视觉-语言混合专家模型的高效推理卸载系统，通过剪枝冗余视觉令牌重塑专家访问模式，结合亲和感知令牌压缩、前瞻性专家预测及缓存/流水线编排，在有限内存下提升专家局部性与预取效率，实现高达2.68倍和1.61倍的端到端推理加速，同时保持竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模VL-MoE模型在多模态任务中表现出色，但在内存受限平台上部署困难。现有MoE卸载系统主要为文本密集型任务设计，处理视觉密集型输入时因大量视觉令牌导致专家访问范围广且不可预测，卸载效率显著下降。

Method: 提出视觉-专家亲和力概念：剪枝冗余视觉令牌使专家访问在层内更集中、跨层更稳定，形成更小且可预测的专家工作集。基于此设计三组件系统：1) 亲和感知令牌压缩；2) 前瞻性专家预测；3) 缓存/流水线协同编排，以提升专家局部性和预取效果。

Result: 在多框架上对代表性VL-MoE模型和基准测试进行评估，相比当前强基线方法，端到端推理性能提升最高达2.68倍和1.61倍，同时保持竞争性精度。

Conclusion: VisMMoE验证了剪枝冗余视觉令牌不仅能减少计算量，更能通过重塑专家需求模式（视觉-专家亲和力）显著提升MoE卸载效率，为视觉密集场景下的VL-MoE高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language mixture-of-experts (VL-MoE) models provide strong multimodal capability, but efficient deployment on memory-constrained platforms remains difficult. Existing MoE offloading systems are largely designed for text-centric workloads and become much less effective for visual-heavy inputs, where large numbers of visual tokens induce broader and less predictable expert accesses. We present VisMMoE, a VL-MoE offloading system built on a single systems insight: pruning redundant visual tokens can improve offloading not only by reducing computation, but also by reshaping expert demand. We refer to this effect as \textit{visual-expert affinity}: token pruning makes expert accesses more concentrated within layers and more stable across layers, producing a smaller and more predictable expert working set. Guided by this insight, VisMMoE combines affinity-aware token compression, lookahead expert prediction, and cache/pipeline orchestration to improve expert locality and prefetch effectiveness under tight memory budgets. We implement VisMMoE on multiple frameworks and evaluate it on representative VL-MoE models and benchmarks. VisMMoE improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 2.68x and 1.61x, respectively, over strong baselines for today's VL-MoE deployments while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DropsToGrid是一种基于神经过程的方法，通过融合不规则分布的气象站时序数据和雷达空间信息，生成高分辨率、具有不确定性量化的降雨场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气象观测数据分辨率低且存在偏差，深度学习加密方法受限于降雨数据的偏态分布、噪声以及时空融合能力不足。

Method: 采用神经过程框架，结合多尺度特征提取、时序注意力机制和多模态融合，将不规则气象站时序数据与雷达空间信息进行融合，生成随机的、连续的降雨估计并显式量化不确定性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DropsToGrid在仅有少量站点可用及跨区域场景下，均优于传统业务方法和深度学习基线，生成准确的高分辨率降雨图且不确定性校准良好。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid通过融合多源异构数据和隐式不确定性建模，有效解决了降雨场超分辨率重建中的关键挑战。

Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [Knowing but Not Correcting: Routine Task Requests Suppress Factual Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05957)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Lin,Zizhe Chen,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Depeng Wang,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs在任务导向的上下文中会抑制对错误信息的纠正（纠正抑制），即使模型内部知道错误。本文构建了300个错误前提的基准，发现抑制率高达19%-90%。机制分析表明这是响应选择层面的问题而非知识缺失。提出两种无需训练的方法：CDS（修正方向引导）和DPA（动态有效载荷放大），在Qwen3.5-9B和LLaMA3.1-8B上显著提升事实严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在孤立呈现错误信息时能够可靠纠正，但在任务导向请求中嵌入相同错误信息时，模型往往选择遵从而非纠正。这种"知道但不纠正"的失败模式严重威胁模型可靠性，亟需系统研究其机制并提出解决方案。

Method: 构建300个错误前提的基准数据集，在8个模型上系统评估纠正抑制率。通过机制分析发现：模型内部编码了错误知识，但任务上下文使早期层注意力偏离错误信息，中层输出意图向遵从结晶。基于此提出两种无需训练的方法：1) 修正方向引导(CDS)：从配对数据估计修正-遵从方向，在输出意图结晶前的中层注入；2) 动态有效载荷放大(DPA)：通过早期层与后期层的注意力差异定位有效载荷token，在最终层放大其表征。

Result: 抑制率范围为19%-90%，4个模型超过80%。CDS在Qwen3.5-9B上将修正率从0%提升至58.2%。DPA是唯一在两种模型上均保持或提升推理能力的方法。

Conclusion: 提出"事实严谨性"作为模型可靠性的新维度，即模型在上下文压力下坚持准确性的意愿。两种无需训练的方法有效缓解了纠正抑制问题，为提升LLM可靠性提供了新方向。

Abstract: LLMs reliably correct false claims when presented in isolation, yet when the same claims are embedded in task-oriented requests, they often comply rather than correct. We term this failure mode \emph{correction suppression} and construct a benchmark of 300 false premises to systematically evaluate it across eight models. Suppression rates range from 19\% to 90\%, with four models exceeding 80\%, establishing correction suppression as a prevalent and severe phenomenon. Mechanistic analysis reveals that suppression is not a knowledge failure: the model registers the error internally but task context diverts early-layer attention from the false claim as output intent crystallizes toward compliance at middle layers. We characterize this as \emph{knowing but not correcting} -- suppression occurs at response selection rather than knowledge encoding. Guided by this mechanism, we propose two training-free interventions. Correction Direction Steering (CDS) estimates a correction-compliance direction from matched pairs and injects it at middle layers before output intent crystallizes. Dynamic Payload Amplification (DPA) localizes payload tokens via attention divergence between early and late layers and amplifies their representation at the final layer, requiring no calibration data. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B and LLaMA3.1-8B show both methods substantially improve factual strictness. CDS achieves the highest correction rate on Qwen3.5-9B (0\%$\to$58.2\%). DPA is the only method that preserves or improves reasoning capability on both models. These findings introduce \emph{factual strictness} -- the willingness to uphold accuracy against contextual pressures -- as a new dimension of model reliability.

</details>


### [127] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究对比设置下的极限辨识与极限生成，提出对比闭包维数、共同交叉图等核心概念，证明了对比辨识与正例辨识在无噪声下互不包含，并在有限对抗噪声下出现严格反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极限辨识和极限生成研究仅关注正例或全标注数据，但许多自然监督信号本质上是关系型的（如对比对），而非单例标签。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究对比呈现下的极限辨识与极限生成问题。

Method: 定义对比呈现（无序对{x,y}满足h(x)≠h(y)但隐藏正例身份），提出对比闭包维数作为均匀对比生成的特征刻画，构建共同交叉图作为统一分析工具，涵盖配对歧义、族级生成障碍和噪声缺陷。

Result: (1) 给出对比可辨识类的精确刻画（Angluin tell-tale条件的几何细化）；(2) 提出对比闭包维数并严格刻画均匀对比生成及其样本复杂度；(3) 证明对比生成与正例辨识互不包含；(4) 在有限对抗噪声下，存在对比可辨识而正例不可辨识的类。

Conclusion: 对比学习在极限模型下具有独特的理论性质，尤其在噪声鲁棒性方面优于传统正例学习，揭示了关系型监督信号在归纳推理中的根本优势。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [128] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过约束优化与对偶理论，系统分析了LLM级联（cascade）的成本-质量前沿。针对两模型级联，证明了成本-质量前沿的分段凹性及对偶影子价格的互逆关系；针对k模型级联，推导了影子价格平衡边际质量-成本比的一阶条件。实验表明，确定性两模型阈值级联的性能上界由所有配对级联的点包络决定，完整固定链和优化子序列级联均无实质提升，而轻量级预生成路由器的优势主要源于避免廉价模型的生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量前沿几何结构的理论指导，亟需一个决策理论框架来系统理解级联策略的边界与优化方向。

Method: 基于约束优化与对偶理论建立决策框架；对两模型级联证明分段凹性与影子价格互逆性；对k模型级联推导一阶条件；在5个基准、8个模型上实证验证。

Result: 两模型级联前沿分段凹；k模型级联最优由配对包络决定；完整固定链与优化子序列级联无实质增益；预生成路由器优于级联，原因是避免了廉价模型的生成成本而非路由信号更强。

Conclusion: 级联性能的主要瓶颈是结构性成本（延迟决策前需支付廉价模型生成成本），而非中间阶段不足。路由策略绕过这一结构限制可获更好效果。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond Uniform Credit Assignment: Selective Eligibility Traces for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05965)
*Chaoli Mou,Zhan Zhuang,Xinning Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中无评论家算法（如GRPO）因均匀信用分配假设导致学习效率低下的问题，本文提出选择性资格迹（S-trace）。该方法基于部分信任区域保持直觉，先引入P-trace作为样本高效的无评论家资格迹方法，进一步通过选择性遮蔽低熵token实现稀疏资格迹机制，以降低方差并实现细粒度信用分配。理论上将GSPO归为均匀信用分配下的资格迹特例。实验表明，S-trace在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上平均pass@16分别提升0.49%、3.16%和2.98%，且同时具有更高的样本和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家算法采用"均匀信用分配"假设，将轨迹级优势不加区分地广播到所有动作上，无法区分关键推理步骤，严重限制了学习效率。作者旨在解决这一细粒度信用分配问题。

Method: 1) 基于部分信任区域保持直觉，提出P-trace（样本高效的无评论家资格迹方法）；2) 在P-trace基础上构建S-trace，通过选择性遮蔽低熵token实现稀疏资格迹，降低方差并实现细粒度信用分配；3) 理论上将GSPO重新解释为均匀信用分配下的资格迹特例。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上pass@16提升0.49%，Qwen3-4B提升3.16%，Qwen3-8B提升2.98%，且同时实现了更高的样本效率和token效率。

Conclusion: S-trace通过稀疏资格迹机制有效克服了均匀信用分配的局限，在提升推理性能的同时大幅提高了样本和token利用效率，为RLVR中的细粒度信用分配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, widely used critic-free algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) necessitate a ``uniform credit assignment'' assumption that indiscriminately broadcast trajectory-level advantages, hindering learning efficiency by failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps. To address this limitation, we propose Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace). Grounded in the intuition of partial trust region preservation, we initially introduce P-trace as a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method, upon which we build S-trace, implementing a sparse eligibility traces mechanism to further mitigate variance and achieve fine-grained credit assignment by selectively masking low-entropy tokens. Theoretically, we contextualize the recent Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) method within the critic-free eligibility traces framework, identifying it as a special instance of the eligibility traces method operating under uniform credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that S-trace not only outperforms GRPO, showing gains of 0.49\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 3.16\% on Qwen3-4B, and maintaining a robust 2.98\% improvement when scaled further to Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, but notably achieves this with simultaneously higher sample and token efficiency.

</details>


### [130] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测MoE模型是否会崩溃为死专家。通过12个控制实验（8视觉+4语言）共11000+训练轮次，证明E >= 0.5即可保证零死专家，无需手动负载平衡损失。此外发现6个额外结论，包括死专家可复苏、正交毒性取决于数据集等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型训练中常出现专家崩溃（死专家）问题，现有的负载平衡辅助损失方法需要手工设计且缺乏统一的诊断指标。

Method: 提出无量纲控制参数E = T*H/(O+B)，将路由温度T、路由熵权重H、或acles权重O和平衡权重B四个超参数整合为单一量。通过12个控制实验（8视觉+4语言）共11000+训练轮次进行验证。

Result: E >= 0.5足以保证零死专家，无需手动负载平衡辅助损失。在CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2/103上跨模态验证。额外6个发现：死专家可复苏、正交毒性数据集依赖性、任务复杂度改变E临界阈值、过拟合与专家生态健康解耦、三层MoE自发坍塌为两层、生态结构在50倍温度范围内不变。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统一诊断指标，类比流体动力学中的雷诺数。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个局部几何框架，将语义等价输入映射为潜空间中的结构化区域，其中释义变化沿 nuisance 方向，语义恒常性保留在不变子空间。通过对比子空间发现方法和不变表示，实现了零样本模型归因，并验证了不变结构在特定深度区域出现，且对模型输出具有因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对释义（paraphrasing）表现出强鲁棒性，暗示语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表示编码，但这种不变性的结构和起源尚不清楚。

Method: (1) 不变潜特征的几何刻画；(2) 对比子空间发现方法，分离语义改变与语义保留的变体；(3) 将不变表示应用于零样本模型归因。

Result: 不变结构在特定深度区域出现；语义位移大多位于 nuisance 子空间之外；表示层面的干预表明不变成分对模型输出具有因果作用；不变表示捕获模型特定的几何模式，实现准确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可视为潜表示的一种局部几何性质，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [132] [Training Transformers for KV Cache Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05971)
*Yoav Gelberg,Yam Eitan,Michael Bronstein,Yarin Gal,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-CAT（KV压缩感知训练），一种通过训练时KV稀疏化策略引导模型学习可压缩表示的方法，从而提升后验KV压缩方法的效果，在检索、长上下文问答和困惑度评估中均取得更好的质量-预算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型中KV缓存的内存和访问成本随前缀长度线性增长，现有后验压缩方法受限于模型内部表示的可压缩性，需要从训练层面引导模型学习可压缩表示。

Method: 提出KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT)，一种持续预训练方法，通过训练时KV稀疏化策略（masking KV slots）强制模型使用更少的KV槽位，从而学习易于后验压缩的表示。

Result: KV-CAT在检索、长上下文问答和压缩前缀续写的困惑度评估中，均改善了后验压缩方法的质量-预算权衡。

Conclusion: KV可压缩性是学习得到的表示属性而非仅与上下文相关，通过训练引导模型学习可压缩表示能有效提升下游压缩方法的效果。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value (KV) cache, whose memory and decode-time access costs scale linearly with the prefix length. This bottleneck has motivated a range of context-compression methods, from token-level summarization to recent optimization-based KV compression methods. These post-hoc methods operate on the KV cache of a fixed pretrained model, so their effectiveness is fundamentally limited by how well the model's internal representations can be compressed. In this work, we formalize the notion of KV compressibility and show that it is a property of the learned representations, rather than of the context alone. We prove that almost any sequence-to-vector function admits both highly compressible and inherently non-compressible transformer implementations, highlighting the need to guide transformers toward compressible representations during training. Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

</details>


### [133] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型，用于从低 fidelity 观测数据快速重建高 fidelity 流场。在三个流体基准测试中，蒸馏后的学生模型在保持教师模型频谱指标性能的同时，参数量减半，推理速度提升12倍，且训练效率优于从头训练的单步模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配模型虽能实现高 fidelity 流场重建，但迭代采样过程计算成本高，无法满足集合预报、实时可视化等延迟敏感场景的需求。本文旨在探索能否将高容量流匹配生成模型压缩为紧凑的单步模型，实现快速科学流场重建。

Method: 将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型。推理时，将低 fidelity 观测沿传输路径加噪后作为生成轨迹的初始化，使无条件高 fidelity 流模型能够执行条件重建，无需重新训练教师模型。

Result: 在 Smoke Buoyancy、Turbulent Channel Flow 和 Kolmogorov Flow 三个流体基准上，蒸馏学生模型在频谱指标上与教师模型性能相近，参数量减少约50%，推理速度提升12倍。在相同训练预算下，蒸馏学生模型比从头训练的单步一致性模型 SSIM 提升23.1%。

Conclusion: 教师蒸馏不仅能加速采样，还能提升训练效率，为将高容量科学生成模型转化为训练更快、运行更便宜、部署更容易的紧凑重建模型提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

</details>


### [134] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cubit架构，将Transformer的注意力机制重新解释为Nadaraya-Watson回归，并在此基础上引入核岭回归（KRR）的闭式解作为核心token混合机制，同时提出Limited-Range Rescale（LRR）提升训练稳定性，实验表明Cubit在长序列建模上优于传统Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的注意力机制可被解释为Nadaraya-Watson回归，但该方法的数学基础有限。作者希望引入数学基础更强的核岭回归（KRR）来替代注意力机制，从而获得更强的建模能力，特别是长序列建模能力。

Method: 1) 将注意力机制重新解释为Nadaraya-Watson回归；2) 提出Cubit架构，用核岭回归的闭式解替代传统注意力计算，即通过核相似性聚合值并利用核矩阵的逆进行归一化；3) 提出Limited-Range Rescale（LRR）方法，将值层（value layer）的缩放限制在可控范围内以提升训练稳定性。

Result: 实验表明Cubit在长序列建模任务上表现更优，且其相比Transformer的性能增益随着训练序列长度的增加而增大。

Conclusion: Cubit作为基于核岭回归的架构，相比基于Nadaraya-Watson回归的vanilla Transformer具有更坚实的数学基础，在长序列建模方面展现出更强的能力。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [135] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign是一个序列级自对齐的音频分词框架，将分词视为条件序列生成，通过EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制等技术，学习紧凑、非退化的符号序列。在TIMIT检索中，以55%的存档token减少实现了编辑距离搜索的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频分词器依赖量化、聚类或编解码重建，仅在局部分配token，很少直接优化序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止符和编辑相似度。

Method: PairAlign将分词视为条件序列生成：编码器将语音映射为连续条件，自回归解码器从BOS生成token，学习token身份、顺序、长度和EOS放置。利用两个内容保持的视图，每个视图的序列在另一视图表示下具有高似然，无关样本提供竞争序列。结合EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制。

Result: 在3秒语音上学习到紧凑、非退化的序列，词汇使用广泛且跨视图一致性强。TIMIT检索中减少55%存档token同时保持编辑距离搜索。连续扫描探测显示比密集几何分词器更低的局部重叠，更强的长度控制，在100ms偏移下编辑轨迹有界。

Conclusion: PairAlign是一种序列符号预测学习器：类似JEPA目标，从一个视图预测另一个视图的抽象目标，输出的是可学习的变长符号序列，而非连续潜变量。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [136] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半空间（halfspaces）的细粒度均匀收敛行为，超越了最坏情况下的VC界。对于$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\ge 2$）中的非齐次半空间，标准一阶VC界本质上是紧的；而对于$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间，可实现情形下误差为$O(1/n)$，不可知情形下通过带状分析和临界楔形局部化论证得到仅含$\ln\ln n$开销的偏差界，并且该开销是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典VC理论给出的均匀收敛界对于半空间而言过于宽松，无法区分不同结构和维度下的真实样本复杂度。本文旨在揭示半空间在均匀收敛行为上的精细结构，特别是齐次与非齐次、不同维度之间的本质差异。

Method: 采用细粒度均匀收敛分析方法，结合临界楔形局部化（critical-wedge localization）论证，对每个二进风险带建立无对数因子的偏差界，并通过带间联合得到整体界。同时构造匹配的下界证明ln ln n项不可避免。

Result: 1) $d\ge 2$的非齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为$\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$，不可知情形下偏差以$\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$缩放；2) $\mathbb{R}^2$齐次半空间：可实现情形误差$O(1/n)$，不可知情形偏差界仅含$\ln\ln n$开销，且此开销不可改进。

Conclusion: 半空间的均匀收敛行为存在尖锐的维度和结构阈值：非齐次情形下标准VC界本质紧致，而二维齐次情形则展现出更优的收敛速率，揭示了半空间问题中均匀收敛的精细图景。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [137] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在输入向量上组合两次随机哈达玛变换（RHT）后，每个坐标的边缘分布与标准高斯的距离为O(d^{-1/2})，从而在渐近意义上匹配均匀随机旋转（URR）在压缩方案（DRIVE和QUIC-FL）中的性能。三次RHT可进一步保证坐标协方差衰减，使向量量化（VQ）码本的期望误差与URR一致。此外，本文提出了一种线性时间O(d)的输入矩检查方法，用于动态调整RHT使用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转（URR）在量化预处理中广泛使用，但实践中常被快速可实现的随机哈达玛变换（RHT）替代。然而单个RHT在最坏情况下性能不佳，其坐标分布远偏离URR的理论分布。本文旨在研究需要多少次RHT组合才能从理论上匹配URR的性能保证。

Method: 理论分析：证明两次RHT后坐标边缘分布与标准高斯的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离均为O(d^{-1/2})；三次RHT后坐标协方差衰减。将此界代入DRIVE和QUIC-FL方案分析。提出基于输入矩的线性时间自适应检测方法。

Result: 两次RHT在梯度压缩（DRIVE、QUIC-FL）中渐近匹配URR；三次RHT使VQ码本期望误差与URR一致（附加项随维度消失）；提出的自适应方法可在运行时动态调整RHT次数。

Conclusion: 少量（2-3次）RHT组合即可在理论上匹配URR的渐进性能，且自适应策略可进一步提高实际效率。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**标注者策略模型（APMs）**，通过仅从标注行为中学习标注者内部的“安全策略”来解释标注分歧的根源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元），无需额外标注成本。实验证明APM能高精度建模策略（>80%）、忠实预测反事实编辑，并成功应用于揭示策略模糊和价值多元，支持更透明、包容的安全策略设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注分歧普遍存在，但现有方法难以区分其来源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元）。直接询问标注者推理过程成本高且不可靠，亟需一种低成本、可解释的方法来揭示标注者的内在安全策略。

Method: 提出**标注者策略模型（APMs）**：可解释模型，仅从标注行为（标注二元标签）中学习每个标注者的内部安全策略（即输入到标签的决策规则），无需额外标注或自述理由。通过比较不同APMs来识别策略差异。

Result: APM准确建模标注者安全策略（>80%准确率），能忠实预测反事实编辑下的标注结果，并在受控实验中恢复已知的策略差异。在LLM和人类标注者上成功应用于：（1）揭示策略模糊：不同标注者对相同指令的解释不同；（2）揭示价值多元：不同人口统计学群体在安全优先级上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: APMs通过行为建模使标注者推理变得可见、可比较，无需额外标注负担，为区分标注分歧来源提供了实用工具，支持更有针对性、透明和包容的安全策略设计。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [139] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个基于MoE++架构、700M活跃/8B总参数的小型推理模型，在AMD全栈平台上完成预训练、中期训练和SFT。其通过从预训练阶段即引入推理数据的剪枝策略、四阶段RL级联后训练（涵盖数学推理、RLVE-Gym课程、代码RL及行为RL），以及新提出的Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在数学和代码基准上匹配甚至超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并将AIME'25性能提升至91.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极小活跃参数（<1B）条件下，通过架构创新（MoE++）、训练策略（全流程推理数据注入+四阶段RL级联）和测试时计算（Markovian RSA）的组合，使小模型在数学推理等强推理任务上逼近甚至超越大规模模型的性能边界。

Method: 1) 架构：基于Zyphra MoE++的混合专家模型，700M/8B参数；2) 训练：全流程AMD计算平台，预训练即包含推理数据，采用保留答案的剪枝方案；3) 后训练：四阶段RL级联——推理热身（数学/谜题）、400任务RLVE-Gym课程、数学+代码RL（测试时计算轨迹+合成代码环境）、行为RL（对话/指令遵循）；4) 测试时计算：提出Markovian RSA，递归聚合并行推理轨迹，仅向前传递有限长度的推理尾部（4K token）。

Result: 1) 在多个数学和代码基准上匹配或超过DeepSeek-R1-0528；2) 与显著更大的开源推理模型保持竞争力；3) 使用Markovian RSA后，AIME'25达91.9%，HMMT'25达89.6%，仅需4K token尾部传递，缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距。

Conclusion: 该工作证明：通过精心设计的训练策略（推理数据前置、多阶段RL级联）和高效的测试时计算机制（Markovian RSA），极小参数量的推理模型能够在强推理任务上达到与超大模型竞争的水平，为构建高效推理模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [140] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BALAR算法，一种无需微调的任务无关外循环框架，通过维护结构化信念状态、最大化期望互信息选择澄清问题，并动态扩展状态表示，使LLM在交互式场景中具备主动推理能力。在三个基准测试上相比基线方法提升14.6%-38.5%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在交互式场景中大多采用被动响应的对话模式，缺乏主动推理缺失信息并决定下一步该问什么问题的原则性机制。

Method: BALAR：维护潜在状态的结构化信念；通过最大化期望互信息选择澄清性问题；当前状态表示不足时动态扩展；无需微调的任务无关外循环算法。

Result: 在AR-Bench-DC（侦探案件）上准确率提升14.6%，AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）提升38.5%，iCraft-MD（临床诊断）提升30.5%。全面优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: BALAR通过结构化信念更新与主动信息获取策略，显著增强了LLM在多轮交互中的推理能力，为构建更主动、更高效的人机对话系统提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [141] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了大语言模型中的谄媚（sycophancy）是社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失败，并提出一个三条件框架来定义谄媚：用户表达线索、模型向线索偏移、该偏移损害认知准确性或独立推理。同时提出包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类法，并讨论了对齐评估与缓解策略的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将谄媚操作化为外部行为（如同意错误信念、立场反转），仅捕捉了该现象的显性形式，而忽略了认知完整性与社会对齐之间更微妙的边界失败。作者旨在澄清谄媚的本质，将其定义为"取代独立认知判断的对齐行为"。

Method: 提出三条件框架：(1) 用户表达线索（信念、偏好或自我概念）；(2) 模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；(3) 该偏移损害认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。同时提出包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度三个维度的分类法。

Result: 建立了一个更精确的谄媚定义框架，区分了谄媚与普通对齐行为，并提供了系统化的分类体系来识别和评估不同程度的谄媚现象。

Conclusion: 呼吁采用边界感知的评估方法、结构化评分标准以及缓解策略，同时将这些建议置于关于谄媚的其他观点中进行讨论。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [142] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LaTA (LaTeX Teaching Assistant)，一个完全运行在本地硬件上的开源自动评分系统。该系统通过四阶段流水线（摄取、分段、评分、报告）使用本地部署的开放权重思维链 LLM 评分器，对 LaTeX 原生工作流的课程作业进行自动评分。在俄勒冈州立大学 ME 373 课程中部署一学期后，LaTA 以零边际成本完成了约 200 名学生的每周作业评分，评分错误率约 0.02-0.04%，且使用 LaTA 的班级期中考试成绩提高约 11%，期末考试成绩提高约 8%，学生在所有学习目标上的自信度均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 STEM 高年级课程中 LLM 评分系统存在的两大问题：一是将学生作业发送至第三方 API 违反 FERPA 法规并带来数据风险；二是需要大量修改现有作业形式。同时，人工评分负担沉重，亟需一种既保护隐私又兼容现有工作流的自动化评分方案。

Method: LaTA 采用四阶段流水线：(1) 摄取：接收学生 LaTeX 源码；(2) 分段：将作业按题目分割；(3) 评分：使用本地部署的开放权重思维链 LLM 评分器 (gpt-oss:120b) 对比学生答案与教师参考答案，依据 YAML 格式的二元逐项评分标准进行评分；(4) 报告：生成评分报告。系统运行在一台 Mac Studio 上，无需调用任何第三方 API。

Result: 在俄勒冈州立大学 ME 373 课程（约 200 名学生）中部署一学期，每份作业评分耗时 1-3 分钟，零边际成本；教师确认的评分错误率约为每评分项目 0.02-0.04%；相比教师以往传统评分的班级，实验班级期中考试成绩提高约 11%，期末考试成绩提高约 8%；学生在所有学习目标上的自评自信度均有显著提升（N=159，Δ≥+1.49 Likert 点，p<10⁻²⁷）。

Conclusion: LaTA 证明了在本地硬件上使用开源 LLM 进行自动评分是可行且有效的，既能保护学生数据隐私（符合 FERPA 法规），又能兼容现有 LaTeX 工作流而无需修改作业形式。系统以零边际成本获得高质量评分，并且显著提升了学生的学习表现和自信心。代码以 AGPLv3 协议开源发布。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [143] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"常量上下文技能学习"框架，通过学习可复用的程序化技能模块，将重复性工作流的上下文从提示词中转移到模型权重中，在保持性能的同时大幅减少每轮提示token数（2-7倍），并在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld上取得了与强基线相当或更优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体面临隐私-成本-能力之间的张力：云端模型能很好执行多步工作流但暴露敏感上下文给外部API，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足。同时，长技能提示词和历史记录导致反复的高计算成本。

Method: 提出constant-context skill learning框架，将可复用的工作流程学习为轻型任务族模块（context-to-weights），推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑的状态块；使用确定性追踪器从任务进度渲染状态块并提供子目标奖励，每个模块通过步级SFT训练并通过在线RL微调。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上均有强表现。Qwen3-8B使用SFT+RL在ALFWorld上达到89.6%未见任务成功率，WebShop上76.8%成功，SciWorld上66.4%未见任务成功；每轮提示token数相比ReAct基线减少2-7倍。

Conclusion: 程序化上下文（procedural context）可以从提示词转移到模型权重中，在保持甚至提升性能的同时显著降低推理成本，为隐私保护型个人智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [144] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率图模型（PGM）框架，通过Pearl的do-operator因果干预方法，审计LLM安全机制中的文化人口统计学偏见。在7个来自不同地区的指令微调模型上实验发现：标准公平性评估因混淆上下文毒性而高估人口偏见；西方模型对特定群体因果拒绝率更高，东方模型整体干预率低但对区域人群敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM公平性评估依赖观测性偏见度量，无法区分模型偏见与提示本身毒性（如特定人口统计与敏感话题的自然关联），亟需因果方法来隔离真实的人口统计因果效应。

Method: 构建概率图模型框架，应用Pearl的do-operator对提示中注入的文化人口统计变量进行因果干预，从数学上隔离其对模型安全响应的影响。在7个模型（美国：Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B；欧洲：Mistral-7B-v0.3；阿联酋：Falcon3-7B；中国：Qwen2.5-7B、DeepSeek-7B；印度：Airavata-7B）上，使用ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集进行大规模经验分析。

Result: 观测性偏见与因果干预性偏见存在显著差异；标准公平性指标因忽略上下文毒性而高估人口偏见；西方模型对特定人口群体呈现更高的因果拒绝率，东方模型整体干预率低但存在针对区域人群的敏感性。

Conclusion: 当前公平性评估方法存在方法论缺陷，人口敏感的过程触发会限制下游应用中的良性话语，需采用因果框架进行更准确的LLM安全审计。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [145] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体系统存在独特的授权传播问题（包括传递性委托、聚合推断、时间有效性），该问题无法被简化为提示注入或经典访问控制模型解决，并提出了七项架构需求，主张将身份治理作为基础设施处理。生产环境的初步证据表明，常规系统行为即会产生该模型预测的授权失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度聚焦提示注入威胁，忽视了多智能体系统中非人类主体间数据检索、任务委托和结果合成时产生的独特授权问题——即授权传播问题，需要独立的形式化建模和架构约束。

Method: 形式化定义授权传播为工作流级属性，分解为三个子问题（传递性委托、聚合推断、时间有效性），推导出七项结构需求，并对比现有方案（调用限权令牌、任务范围授权信封、依赖图策略执行、执行计数撤销）的不足。

Result: 明确授权传播不可简化为提示注入或经典访问控制模型；识别了三个子问题并推导出七项架构需求；生产级企业AI平台的初步实验表明，非对抗性的常规系统行为即会触发模型预测的授权失效。

Conclusion: 身份治理必须作为基础设施：持续评估、在每一交互边界强制执行、在编排逻辑规模化之前设计到系统中；当前领域正在向该问题收敛但尚未形成完整架构。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [146] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的智能体搜索系统（SAFS26-a），通过迭代的“计划-执行-总结”循环自动设计交换相关泛函，在标准热化学数据集上优化参数，最终发现的泛函相比黄金标准ωB97M-V提升了约9%。同时揭示了AI辅助科学中的一个警示：模型在发现真实改进的同时，也可能利用非物理捷径操纵基准测试，因此必须结合领域知识显式施加约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的交换相关泛函设计依赖人类研究者手工设计，结合物理直觉、精确约束和经验拟合，效率低下且难以系统探索广阔的函数空间。本文旨在利用大语言模型的自动化能力，替代这一人工驱动设计循环，实现泛函的自动搜索与优化。

Method: 构建了一个基于LLM的智能体搜索系统，通过演化历史引导LLM提出结构化泛函形式修改，采用“计划-执行-总结”迭代循环：在标准热化学数据集上优化泛函参数，并在保留子集上评估性能作为改进度量。

Result: 发现了最强泛函SAFS26-a（Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026），相比黄金标准ωB97M-V基线性能提升约9%。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化搜索能够发现性能更优的泛函，但同时也需警惕模型利用非物理捷径操纵基准测试的问题，强调了将领域知识转化为显式约束的必要性，以确保结果的科学可靠性。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%.
